=== FILE: firestore.rules ===
rules_version = '2';
service cloud.firestore {
  match /databases/{database}/documents {

    function me() {
      return get(/databases/$(database)/documents/users/$(request.auth.uid)).data;
    }
    function ok() {
      return request.auth != null && me().get('approved', true) == true;
    }
    function isAdmin() {
      return request.auth != null && me().get('admin', false) == true;
    }
    function inRoom(r) {
      return request.auth.uid in get(/databases/$(database)/documents/rooms/$(r)).data.members;
    }
    function sizeOk() {
      return request.resource.data.text is string
        && request.resource.data.text.size() <= 2000
        && (!('image' in request.resource.data) || request.resource.data.image.size() < 800000)
        && (!('file' in request.resource.data) || request.resource.data.file.size() < 800000);
    }
    function editOk() {
      return resource.data.uid == request.auth.uid
        && request.resource.data.diff(resource.data).affectedKeys().hasOnly(['text','edited'])
        && request.resource.data.text is string
        && request.resource.data.text.size() <= 2000;
    }
    function readOk() {
      return request.resource.data.diff(resource.data).affectedKeys().hasOnly(['readBy'])
        && request.auth.uid in request.resource.data.readBy
        && request.resource.data.readBy.hasAll(resource.data.get('readBy', []));
    }
    function isLawyer() {
      return request.auth != null && me().get('dept', '') == 'legal';
    }
    function canDelete() {
      return resource.data.uid == request.auth.uid || isAdmin();
    }

    match /contracts/{c} {
      allow read: if ok() && (resource.data.authorUid == request.auth.uid || isLawyer() || isAdmin());
      allow create: if ok()
                    && request.resource.data.authorUid == request.auth.uid
                    && request.resource.data.status == 'new'
                    && request.resource.data.title is string
                    && request.resource.data.title.size() <= 120
                    && request.resource.data.get('fileMeta', []).size() <= 5;
      allow update: if ok() && (
        ((isLawyer() || isAdmin())
          && request.resource.data.diff(resource.data).affectedKeys()
               .hasOnly(['status','lawyerComment','decidedBy','decidedTime','fileMeta']))
        ||
        (resource.data.authorUid == request.auth.uid
          && resource.data.status == 'revision'
          && request.resource.data.status == 'new'
          && request.resource.data.diff(resource.data).affectedKeys()
               .hasOnly(['status','round','note','fileMeta'])));
      allow delete: if ok() && ((resource.data.authorUid == request.auth.uid && resource.data.status == 'new') || isAdmin());

      match /files/{f} {
        allow read: if ok() && (resource.data.authorUid == request.auth.uid || isLawyer() || isAdmin());
        allow create: if ok()
                      && request.resource.data.uid == request.auth.uid
                      && request.resource.data.b64 is string
                      && request.resource.data.b64.size() < 800000
                      && ((request.resource.data.by == 'author' && request.resource.data.authorUid == request.auth.uid)
                          || (request.resource.data.by == 'lawyer' && (isLawyer() || isAdmin())));
        allow delete: if ok() && (resource.data.authorUid == request.auth.uid || isAdmin());
      }
    }

    match /messages/{id} {
      allow read: if ok();
      allow create: if ok() && request.resource.data.uid == request.auth.uid && sizeOk();
      allow update: if ok() && (editOk() || readOk());
      allow delete: if ok() && canDelete();
    }

    match /users/{u} {
      allow read: if request.auth != null && (request.auth.uid == u || ok());
      allow create: if request.auth != null && request.auth.uid == u
                    && request.resource.data.approved == false;
      allow update: if request.auth != null && (
        (request.auth.uid == u
          && !request.resource.data.diff(resource.data).affectedKeys().hasAny(['approved','admin','dept','office','position']))
        ||
        (isAdmin() && request.resource.data.diff(resource.data).affectedKeys().hasOnly(['approved','dept','office','position'])));
    }

    match /meta/{d} {
      allow read: if ok();
      allow write: if ok()
                   && request.resource.data.keys().hasOnly(['lastText','lastTime','lastName','lastUid','unread']);
    }

    match /alerts/{a} {
      allow read: if ok();
      allow write: if false;
    }

    match /rooms/{r} {
      allow read: if ok() && (resource == null || request.auth.uid in resource.data.members);
      allow create: if ok() && request.auth.uid in request.resource.data.members;
      allow update: if ok() && (
        (request.auth.uid in resource.data.members
          && request.resource.data.diff(resource.data).affectedKeys()
               .hasOnly(['members','lastText','lastTime','lastName','lastUid','unread'])
          && request.resource.data.members.hasAll(resource.data.members))
        ||
        (request.resource.data.diff(resource.data).affectedKeys().hasOnly(['members'])
          && request.auth.uid in request.resource.data.members
          && request.resource.data.members.hasAll(resource.data.members))
        ||
        (isAdmin()
          && request.resource.data.diff(resource.data).affectedKeys().hasOnly(['name','photo'])
          && request.resource.data.get('name', '') is string
          && request.resource.data.get('name', '').size() <= 60
          && request.resource.data.get('photo', '').size() < 300000));
      allow delete: if ok() && isAdmin();

      match /messages/{m} {
        allow read: if ok() && inRoom(r);
        allow create: if ok() && inRoom(r) && request.resource.data.uid == request.auth.uid && sizeOk();
        allow update: if ok() && inRoom(r) && (editOk() || readOk());
        allow delete: if ok() && inRoom(r) && canDelete();
      }
    }
  }
}

// Картинки лежат в проекте текстом (base64), перед сборкой превращаем их в PNG
for ((s, d) in listOf(
    "assets-src/release.keystore.b64" to "build/keystore/release.keystore",
    "assets-src/logo.b64" to "src/main/res/drawable-nodpi/logo.png",
    "assets-src/icon.b64" to "src/main/res/drawable-nodpi/ic_launcher_foreground.png"
)) {
    val f = file(s)
    if (f.exists()) {
        val o = file(d)
        o.parentFile.mkdirs()
        o.writeBytes(Base64.getMimeDecoder().decode(f.readText().trim()))
    }
}

class MainActivity : ComponentActivity() {
    override fun onCreate(savedInstanceState: Bundle?) {
        super.onCreate(savedInstanceState)
        window.statusBarColor = Navy.toArgb()
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 26) {
            getSystemService(NotificationManager::class.java).createNotificationChannel(
                NotificationChannel("urgent", "Срочные уведомления", NotificationManager.IMPORTANCE_HIGH))
        }
        setContent { AppTheme { Root() } }
    }
}

@Composable
fun AppTheme(content: @Composable () -> Unit) {
    val dark = isSystemInDarkTheme()
    MaterialTheme(
        colorScheme = if (dark) darkColorScheme(
            primary = Color(0xFF6FA8E8), background = Color(0xFF0E1726), surface = Color(0xFF18243A)
        ) else lightColorScheme(
            primary = Ocean, background = Color(0xFFEAF2F8), surface = Color.White
        ),
        content = content
    )
}

// ---------- помощники ----------

fun sameDay(a: Long, b: Long): Boolean {
    val x = Calendar.getInstance().apply { timeInMillis = a }
    val y = Calendar.getInstance().apply { timeInMillis = b }
    return x.get(Calendar.YEAR) == y.get(Calendar.YEAR) &&
        x.get(Calendar.DAY_OF_YEAR) == y.get(Calendar.DAY_OF_YEAR)
}

fun dayLabel(t: Long): String {
    val now = System.currentTimeMillis()
    return when {
        sameDay(t, now) -> "Сегодня"
        sameDay(t, now - 86_400_000L) -> "Вчера"
        else -> SimpleDateFormat("d MMMM yyyy", Locale.getDefault()).format(Date(t))
    }
}

fun staff() = Users.values.filter { it.approved }

fun mentionText(text: String, color: Color): AnnotatedString {
    val names = staff().map { it.name }.filter { it.isNotBlank() }.sortedByDescending { it.length }
    if (!text.contains('@')) return AnnotatedString(text)
    return buildAnnotatedString {
        var i = 0
        while (i < text.length) {
            var hit: String? = null
            if (text[i] == '@' && (i == 0 || text[i - 1].isWhitespace())) {
                hit = names.firstOrNull { text.startsWith(it, i + 1) }
            }
            if (hit != null) {
                withStyle(SpanStyle(color = color, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)) { append("@" + hit) }
                i += hit.length + 1
            } else { append(text[i]); i++ }
        }
    }
}

fun isOnline(u: User?, now: Long) = u != null && now - u.lastSeen < 100_000L

fun decodePhoto(b64: String): ImageBitmap? {
    if (b64.isEmpty()) return null
    return try {
        val b = Base64.decode(b64, Base64.DEFAULT)
        BitmapFactory.decodeByteArray(b, 0, b.size)?.asImageBitmap()
    } catch (e: Exception) { null }
}

fun encodePhoto(ctx: Context, uri: Uri): String? {
    try {
        val opts = BitmapFactory.Options().apply { inJustDecodeBounds = true }
        ctx.contentResolver.openInputStream(uri)?.use { BitmapFactory.decodeStream(it, null, opts) }
        var sample = 1
        while (opts.outWidth / sample > 1200) sample *= 2
        val bmp = ctx.contentResolver.openInputStream(uri)?.use {
            BitmapFactory.decodeStream(it, null, BitmapFactory.Options().apply { inSampleSize = sample })
        } ?: return null
        val side = minOf(bmp.width, bmp.height)
        val sq = Bitmap.createBitmap(bmp, (bmp.width - side) / 2, (bmp.height - side) / 2, side, side)
        val out = Bitmap.createScaledBitmap(sq, 256, 256, true)
        val bos = ByteArrayOutputStream()
        out.compress(Bitmap.CompressFormat.JPEG, 80, bos)
        return Base64.encodeToString(bos.toByteArray(), Base64.NO_WRAP)
    } catch (e: Exception) {
        return null
    }
}

fun encodeChatImage(ctx: Context, uri: Uri): String? {
    try {
        val opts = BitmapFactory.Options().apply { inJustDecodeBounds = true }
        ctx.contentResolver.openInputStream(uri)?.use { BitmapFactory.decodeStream(it, null, opts) }
        var sample = 1
        while (opts.outWidth / sample > 2000) sample *= 2
        val bmp = ctx.contentResolver.openInputStream(uri)?.use {
            BitmapFactory.decodeStream(it, null, BitmapFactory.Options().apply { inSampleSize = sample })
        } ?: return null
        val k = 1000f / maxOf(bmp.width, bmp.height)
        val out = if (k < 1f) Bitmap.createScaledBitmap(bmp, (bmp.width * k).toInt(), (bmp.height * k).toInt(), true) else bmp
        var q = 75
        var bytes: ByteArray
        do {
            val bos = ByteArrayOutputStream()
            out.compress(Bitmap.CompressFormat.JPEG, q, bos)
            bytes = bos.toByteArray()
            q -= 10
        } while (bytes.size > 600_000 && q > 20)
        return Base64.encodeToString(bytes, Base64.NO_WRAP)
    } catch (e: Exception) {
        return null
    }
}

// документ: имя и содержимое (до 600 КБ, иначе null)
fun readDoc(ctx: Context, uri: Uri): Pair<String, String>? {
    try {
        var name = "файл"
        var size = 0L
        ctx.contentResolver.query(uri, null, null, null, null)?.use { c ->
            if (c.moveToFirst()) {
                val i = c.getColumnIndex(OpenableColumns.DISPLAY_NAME)
                if (i >= 0) name = c.getString(i) ?: name
                val j = c.getColumnIndex(OpenableColumns.SIZE)
                if (j >= 0) size = c.getLong(j)
            }
        }
        if (size > 600_000) return null
        val bytes = ctx.contentResolver.openInputStream(uri)?.use { it.readBytes() } ?: return null
        if (bytes.size > 600_000) return null
        return name to Base64.encodeToString(bytes, Base64.NO_WRAP)
    } catch (e: Exception) {
        return null
    }
}

fun openDoc(ctx: Context, name: String, b64: String) {
    try {
        val dir = java.io.File(ctx.cacheDir, "docs").apply { mkdirs() }
        val f = java.io.File(dir, name.replace('/', '_'))
        f.writeBytes(Base64.decode(b64, Base64.DEFAULT))
        val uri = FileProvider.getUriForFile(ctx, ctx.packageName + ".fileprovider", f)
        val ext = name.substringAfterLast('.', "").lowercase()
        val mime = android.webkit.MimeTypeMap.getSingleton().getMimeTypeFromExtension(ext) ?: "*/*"
        val i = Intent(Intent.ACTION_VIEW).apply {
            setDataAndType(uri, mime)
            addFlags(Intent.FLAG_GRANT_READ_URI_PERMISSION)
        }
        ctx.startActivity(Intent.createChooser(i, "Открыть"))
    } catch (e: Exception) {
        Toast.makeText(ctx, "Не удалось открыть файл", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
    }
}

fun openDirect(uid: String, u: User, onOpen: (String) -> Unit) {
    val id = listOf(uid, u.uid).sorted().joinToString("_")
    val ref = Firebase.firestore.collection("rooms").document(id)
    ref.get().addOnSuccessListener { d ->
        if (d.exists()) onOpen(id)
        else ref.set(mapOf("type" to "direct", "name" to "", "members" to listOf(uid, u.uid)))
            .addOnSuccessListener { onOpen(id) }
    }
}

fun roomTitle(r: Room, uid: String): String = when {
    r.id == "general" -> "Общий чат"
    r.type == "direct" -> r.members.firstOrNull { it != uid }?.let { Users[it]?.name } ?: "Личный чат"
    else -> r.name.ifBlank { "Группа" }
}

fun msgCol(r: Room) =
    if (r.id == "general") Firebase.firestore.collection("messages")
    else Firebase.firestore.collection("rooms").document(r.id).collection("messages")

fun listTime(t: Long): String {
    if (t == 0L) return ""
    val now = System.currentTimeMillis()
    val loc = Locale.getDefault()
    return when {
        sameDay(t, now) -> SimpleDateFormat("HH:mm", loc).format(Date(t))
        sameDay(t, now - 86_400_000L) -> "вчера"
        now - t < 6 * 86_400_000L -> SimpleDateFormat("EEEE", loc).format(Date(t)).lowercase()
        else -> SimpleDateFormat("dd.MM.yyyy", loc).format(Date(t))
    }
}

// после отправки: обновляем «последнее сообщение» и счётчики непрочитанных у остальных
fun bumpLast(room: Room, uid: String, myName: String, text: String, time: Long) {
    val short = text.take(120)
    if (room.id == "general") {
        val unread = staff().map { it.uid }.filter { it != uid }.associateWith { FieldValue.increment(1) }
        Firebase.firestore.collection("meta").document("general").set(
            mapOf("lastText" to short, "lastTime" to time, "lastName" to myName,
                "lastUid" to uid, "unread" to unread), SetOptions.merge())
    } else {
        val upd = hashMapOf<String, Any>("lastText" to short, "lastTime" to time,
            "lastName" to myName, "lastUid" to uid)
        room.members.filter { it != uid }.forEach { upd["unread.$it"] = FieldValue.increment(1) }
        Firebase.firestore.collection("rooms").document(room.id).update(upd)
    }
}

fun markRead(room: Room, uid: String) {
    if (room.id == "general") {
        Firebase.firestore.collection("meta").document("general")
            .set(mapOf("unread" to mapOf(uid to 0)), SetOptions.merge())
    } else {
        Firebase.firestore.collection("rooms").document(room.id).update("unread.$uid", 0)
    }
}

@Composable
fun rememberNow(): Long {
    val s = produceState(System.currentTimeMillis()) {
        while (true) { delay(30_000); value = System.currentTimeMillis() }
    }
    return s.value
}

// ---------- общие элементы ----------

@Composable
fun Avatar(name: String, photo: String, size: Dp, online: Boolean? = null) {
    val bmp = remember(photo) { decodePhoto(photo) }
    val bg = NameColors[(name.hashCode() and 0x7fffffff) % NameColors.size]
    Box(Modifier.size(size)) {
        Box(Modifier.fillMaxSize().clip(CircleShape).background(bg), contentAlignment = Alignment.Center) {
            if (bmp != null) {
                Image(bmp, null, Modifier.fillMaxSize(), contentScale = ContentScale.Crop)
            } else {
                Text(name.take(1).uppercase(), color = Color.White,
                    fontSize = (size.value / 2.2f).sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
            }
        }
        if (online != null) {
            Box(Modifier.size(size / 3.4f).align(Alignment.BottomEnd).clip(CircleShape)
                .background(Color.White).padding(2.dp).clip(CircleShape)
                .background(if (online) Color(0xFF2ECC71) else Color(0xFF9E9E9E)))
        }
    }
}

@OptIn(ExperimentalMaterial3Api::class)
@Composable
fun Bar(onBack: (() -> Unit)? = null, actions: @Composable RowScope.() -> Unit = {}, title: @Composable () -> Unit) {
    TopAppBar(
        title = title,
        navigationIcon = {
            if (onBack != null) TextButton(onClick = onBack,
                colors = ButtonDefaults.textButtonColors(contentColor = Color.White)) { Text("←", fontSize = 24.sp) }
        },
        actions = actions,
        colors = TopAppBarDefaults.topAppBarColors(
            containerColor = Navy, titleContentColor = Color.White, actionIconContentColor = Color.White)
    )
}

@Composable
fun Logo(modifier: Modifier = Modifier) {
    Image(painterResource(R.drawable.logo), "ВМС Групп", modifier.clip(CircleShape),
        contentScale = ContentScale.Fit)
}

// ---------- корень ----------

@Composable
fun TabBar(current: Screen, onSelect: (Screen) -> Unit) {
    NavigationBar(containerColor = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.surface) {
        for ((scr, icon, label) in listOf<Triple<Screen, String, String>>(
            Triple(Screen.Rooms, "💬", "Чаты"), Triple(Screen.Alerts, "🚨", "Срочные"),
            Triple(Screen.Tariff, "🚢", "Расчёт"),
            Triple(Screen.People, "🏢", "Отделы"), Triple(Screen.Profile, "👤", "Профиль"))) {
            NavigationBarItem(selected = current == scr, onClick = { onSelect(scr) },
                icon = { Text(icon, fontSize = 22.sp) }, label = { Text(label, fontSize = 12.sp) })
        }
    }
}

@Composable
fun PendingScreen(onLogout: () -> Unit) {
    Column(Modifier.fillMaxSize().background(MaterialTheme.colorScheme.background).padding(28.dp),
        horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally, verticalArrangement = Arrangement.Center) {
        Logo(Modifier.fillMaxWidth(0.5f).aspectRatio(1f))
        Spacer(Modifier.height(18.dp))
        Text("Заявка отправлена", fontSize = 22.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
        Spacer(Modifier.height(8.dp))
        Text("Администратор должен подтвердить ваш доступ.\nПосле этого чат откроется сам.",
            textAlign = TextAlign.Center, color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onBackground.copy(alpha = 0.7f))
        Spacer(Modifier.height(18.dp))
        OutlinedButton(onClick = onLogout, shape = RoundedCornerShape(12.dp)) { Text("Выйти") }
    }
}

@Suppress("UNCHECKED_CAST")
@Composable
fun Root() {
    var signedIn by remember { mutableStateOf(Firebase.auth.currentUser != null) }
    if (!signedIn) { AuthScreen { signedIn = true }; return }
    val me = Firebase.auth.currentUser ?: return
    val uid = me.uid
    UpdateChecker()
    val myName = me.displayName ?: me.email ?: "Менеджер"
    val db = Firebase.firestore
    val rooms = remember { mutableStateListOf<Room>() }
    var general by remember { mutableStateOf(General) }
    var screen by remember { mutableStateOf<Screen>(Screen.Rooms) }
    var status by remember { mutableStateOf("loading") }
    var isAdmin by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    val notifPerm = rememberLauncherForActivityResult(ActivityResultContracts.RequestPermission()) {}

    // свой профиль: новый сотрудник ждёт подтверждения администратора
    DisposableEffect(uid) {
        val ref = db.collection("users").document(uid)
        ref.get().addOnSuccessListener { d ->
            val em = Firebase.auth.currentUser?.email ?: ""
            if (d.exists()) ref.set(mapOf("name" to myName, "email" to em), SetOptions.merge())
            else ref.set(mapOf("name" to myName, "email" to em, "approved" to false, "lastSeen" to 0L))
        }
        val reg = ref.addSnapshotListener { d, _ ->
            if (d != null && d.exists()) {
                status = if (d.getBoolean("approved") == false) "pending" else "ok"
                isAdmin = d.getBoolean("admin") == true
            }
        }
        onDispose { reg.remove() }
    }
    DisposableEffect(uid, status) {
        if (status != "ok") {
            onDispose { }
        } else {
            val reg = db.collection("users").addSnapshotListener { s, _ ->
                s?.documents?.forEach {
                    Users[it.id] = User(it.id, it.getString("name") ?: "?", it.getString("photo") ?: "",
                        it.getLong("lastSeen") ?: 0L, it.getBoolean("approved") ?: true,
                        it.getBoolean("admin") ?: false, it.getString("dept") ?: "",
                        it.getString("office") ?: "", it.getString("position") ?: "",
                        it.getString("email") ?: "", it.getString("phone") ?: "")
                }
            }
            onDispose { reg.remove() }
        }
    }
    DisposableEffect(uid, status) {
        if (status != "ok") {
            onDispose { }
        } else {
            val reg = db.collection("rooms").whereArrayContains("members", uid)
                .addSnapshotListener { s, _ ->
                    rooms.clear()
                    s?.documents?.forEach { d ->
                        rooms.add(Room(d.id, d.getString("name") ?: "",
                            (d.get("members") as? List<String>) ?: emptyList(),
                            d.getString("type") ?: "group",
                            d.getString("lastText") ?: "", d.getLong("lastTime") ?: 0L,
                            d.getString("lastName") ?: "", d.getString("lastUid") ?: "",
                            ((d.get("unread") as? Map<String, Any>)?.get(uid) as? Number)?.toInt() ?: 0,
                            d.getString("photo") ?: ""))
                    }
                }
            onDispose { reg.remove() }
        }
    }
    DisposableEffect(uid, status) {
        if (status != "ok") {
            onDispose { }
        } else {
            val reg = db.collection("meta").document("general").addSnapshotListener { d, _ ->
                if (d != null && d.exists()) {
                    general = General.copy(
                        lastText = d.getString("lastText") ?: "", lastTime = d.getLong("lastTime") ?: 0L,
                        lastName = d.getString("lastName") ?: "", lastUid = d.getString("lastUid") ?: "",
                        unread = ((d.get("unread") as? Map<String, Any>)?.get(uid) as? Number)?.toInt() ?: 0)
                }
            }
            onDispose { reg.remove() }
        }
    }

    // push-уведомления о срочных событиях
    LaunchedEffect(status) {
        if (status == "ok") {
            FirebaseMessaging.getInstance().subscribeToTopic("managers")
            if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= 33) notifPerm.launch(Manifest.permission.POST_NOTIFICATIONS)
        }
    }

    // «в сети»: пока приложение открыто, раз в 40 секунд обновляем отметку времени
    val owner = LocalLifecycleOwner.current
    var foreground by remember { mutableStateOf(true) }
    DisposableEffect(owner) {
        val obs = LifecycleEventObserver { _, e ->
            if (e == Lifecycle.Event.ON_RESUME) foreground = true
            else if (e == Lifecycle.Event.ON_PAUSE) foreground = false
        }
        owner.lifecycle.addObserver(obs)
        onDispose { owner.lifecycle.removeObserver(obs) }
    }
    LaunchedEffect(uid, foreground, status) {
        if (status == "ok") {
            val ref = db.collection("users").document(uid)
            if (foreground) {
                while (true) {
                    ref.set(mapOf("lastSeen" to System.currentTimeMillis()), SetOptions.merge())
                    delay(40_000)
                }
            } else {
                ref.set(mapOf("lastSeen" to 0L), SetOptions.merge())
            }
        }
    }

    BackHandler(screen != Screen.Rooms) {
        screen = when (val s = screen) {
            is Screen.Members -> Screen.Chat(s.id)
            is Screen.Invite -> Screen.Members(s.id)
            is Screen.Dept -> Screen.People
            else -> Screen.Rooms
        }
    }

    fun find(id: String): Room? = if (id == "general") general else rooms.find { it.id == id }
    fun logout() { Firebase.auth.signOut(); signedIn = false; screen = Screen.Rooms; status = "loading" }

    if (status == "loading") {
        Box(Modifier.fillMaxSize(), contentAlignment = Alignment.Center) { CircularProgressIndicator() }
        return
    }
    if (status == "pending") { PendingScreen(onLogout = { logout() }); return }

    val topLevel = screen == Screen.Rooms || screen == Screen.Alerts ||
        screen == Screen.People || screen == Screen.Profile || screen == Screen.Tariff
    Scaffold(bottomBar = { if (topLevel) TabBar(screen) { screen = it } }) { outer ->
        Box(Modifier.fillMaxSize().padding(outer)) {
            when (val s = screen) {
                Screen.Rooms -> RoomsScreen(uid, myName, general, rooms,
                    onOpen = { screen = Screen.Chat(it.id) }, onProfile = { screen = Screen.Profile },
                    onDirect = { screen = Screen.NewDirect }, onGroup = { screen = Screen.NewGroup },
                    onAssistant = { screen = Screen.Assistant }, onLegal = { screen = Screen.Legal },
                    onMail = { screen = Screen.Mail("") })
                Screen.Alerts -> AlertsScreen()
                Screen.Tariff -> TariffScreen()
                Screen.Assistant -> AssistantScreen(isAdmin, onBack = { screen = Screen.Rooms })
                Screen.Legal -> LegalScreen(uid, myName, isAdmin, onBack = { screen = Screen.Rooms })
                is Screen.Mail -> MailScreen(s.to, onBack = { screen = Screen.Rooms })
                Screen.People -> DeptsScreen(uid, isAdmin, onChat = { screen = Screen.Chat(it) },
                    onDept = { screen = Screen.Dept(it) }, onMail = { screen = Screen.Mail(it) })
                is Screen.Dept -> DeptScreen(s.key, uid, isAdmin, onBack = { screen = Screen.People },
                    onChat = { screen = Screen.Chat(it) }, onLegal = { screen = Screen.Legal },
                    onMail = { screen = Screen.Mail(it) })
                Screen.Profile -> ProfileScreen(uid, myName, onBack = null, onLogout = { logout() })
                Screen.NewDirect -> DirectScreen(uid, myName, onBack = { screen = Screen.Rooms },
                    onOpen = { screen = Screen.Chat(it) })
                Screen.NewGroup -> GroupScreen(uid, onBack = { screen = Screen.Rooms },
                    onCreated = { screen = Screen.Chat(it) })
                is Screen.Chat -> {
                    val r = find(s.id)
                    if (r == null) Box(Modifier.fillMaxSize(), contentAlignment = Alignment.Center) { CircularProgressIndicator() }
                    else ChatScreen(r, uid, myName, targets = listOf(general) + rooms, onBack = { screen = Screen.Rooms },
                        onMembers = { screen = Screen.Members(r.id) },
                        onOpenRoom = { screen = Screen.Chat(it) }, onMailTo = { screen = Screen.Mail(it) })
                }
                is Screen.Members -> {
                    val r = find(s.id)
                    if (r != null) MembersScreen(r, uid, isAdmin, onBack = { screen = Screen.Chat(r.id) },
                        onInvite = { screen = Screen.Invite(r.id) }, onDeleted = { screen = Screen.Rooms })
                }
                is Screen.Invite -> {
                    val r = find(s.id)
                    if (r != null) InviteScreen(r, uid, onBack = { screen = Screen.Members(r.id) })
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

// ---------- срочные уведомления и участники ----------

@Composable
fun AlertsScreen() {
    val alerts = remember { mutableStateListOf<Alert>() }
    DisposableEffect(Unit) {
        val reg = Firebase.firestore.collection("alerts").orderBy("time", Query.Direction.DESCENDING).limit(100)
            .addSnapshotListener { s, _ ->
                alerts.clear()
                s?.documents?.forEach {
                    alerts.add(Alert(it.getString("title") ?: "", it.getString("body") ?: "",
                        it.getBoolean("urgent") ?: false, it.getLong("time") ?: 0L))
                }
            }
        onDispose { reg.remove() }
    }
    Scaffold(topBar = { Bar { Text("Срочные уведомления", fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold) } }) { pad ->
        Box(Modifier.fillMaxSize().padding(pad)) {
            if (alerts.isEmpty()) {
                Text("Пока нет срочных уведомлений.\nЗдесь появятся письма о выпуске контейнеров и другие важные события.",
                    Modifier.align(Alignment.Center).padding(28.dp), textAlign = TextAlign.Center,
                    color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onBackground.copy(alpha = 0.55f))
            }
            LazyColumn(Modifier.fillMaxSize(), contentPadding = PaddingValues(12.dp),
                verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)) {
                items(alerts) { a ->
                    val txt = if (a.urgent) Color(0xFF3B0D0D) else Color.Unspecified
                    Card(Modifier.fillMaxWidth(), colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(
                        containerColor = if (a.urgent) Color(0xFFFDECEA) else MaterialTheme.colorScheme.surface)) {
                        Column(Modifier.padding(14.dp)) {
                            Text((if (a.urgent) "🔴 " else "") + a.title, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                                fontSize = 16.sp, color = txt)
                            Text(a.body, fontSize = 14.sp, color = txt, modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 4.dp))
                            Text(dayLabel(a.time) + ", " + SimpleDateFormat("HH:mm", Locale.getDefault()).format(Date(a.time)),
                                fontSize = 12.sp, color = if (a.urgent) Color(0xFF7A4A4A) else MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurface.copy(alpha = 0.5f),
                                modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 6.dp))
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
fun PeopleScreen(uid: String, isAdmin: Boolean, onChat: (String) -> Unit) {
    val ctx = LocalContext.current
    val now = rememberNow()
    val pending = Users.values.filter { !it.approved }.sortedBy { it.name.lowercase() }
    val list = staff().filter { it.uid != uid }
        .sortedWith(compareByDescending<User> { isOnline(it, now) }.thenBy { it.name.lowercase() })
    val online = list.count { isOnline(it, now) }
    fun approve(u: User) {
        Firebase.firestore.collection("users").document(u.uid).update("approved", true)
            .addOnFailureListener { Toast.makeText(ctx, "Не удалось подтвердить", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show() }
    }
    Scaffold(topBar = { Bar { Text("Участники", fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold) } }) { pad ->
        LazyColumn(Modifier.fillMaxSize().padding(pad)) {
            if (isAdmin && pending.isNotEmpty()) {
                item {
                    Text("Заявки на вход: ${pending.size}", Modifier.padding(16.dp),
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Medium, color = Color(0xFFC62828))
                }
                items(pending) { u ->
                    Row(Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(horizontal = 16.dp, vertical = 6.dp),
                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                        Avatar(u.name, u.photo, 42.dp)
                        Spacer(Modifier.width(12.dp))
                        Text(u.name, Modifier.weight(1f), fontSize = 16.sp)
                        Button(onClick = { approve(u) }, shape = RoundedCornerShape(10.dp),
                            colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(containerColor = Ocean)) {
                            Text("Подтвердить", color = Color.White)
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            item {
                Text("В сети: $online из ${list.size}", Modifier.padding(16.dp),
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Medium, color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.primary)
            }
            if (list.isEmpty()) item { Text("Других сотрудников пока нет", Modifier.padding(16.dp)) }
            items(list) { u ->
                val on = isOnline(u, now)
                Row(Modifier.fillMaxWidth().clickable { openDirect(uid, u, onChat) }
                    .padding(horizontal = 16.dp, vertical = 8.dp), verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                    Avatar(u.name, u.photo, 46.dp, on)
                    Spacer(Modifier.width(14.dp))
                    Column {
                        Text(u.name, fontSize = 17.sp)
                        Text(if (on) "в сети" else "не в сети", fontSize = 13.sp,
                            color = if (on) Color(0xFF2E9E5B) else MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onBackground.copy(alpha = 0.5f))
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

// ---------- вход ----------

@Composable
fun AuthScreen(onDone: () -> Unit) {
    var register by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var name by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
    var email by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
    var pass by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
    var error by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
    var busy by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }

    fun submit() {
        error = ""
        if (register && name.isBlank()) { error = "Введите имя"; return }
        busy = true
        val auth = Firebase.auth
        if (register) {
            auth.createUserWithEmailAndPassword(email.trim(), pass)
                .addOnSuccessListener { r ->
                    r.user?.updateProfile(userProfileChangeRequest { displayName = name.trim() })
                        ?.addOnCompleteListener { onDone() }
                }
                .addOnFailureListener { busy = false; error = it.message ?: "Ошибка регистрации" }
        } else {
            auth.signInWithEmailAndPassword(email.trim(), pass)
                .addOnSuccessListener { onDone() }
                .addOnFailureListener { busy = false; error = it.message ?: "Ошибка входа" }
        }
    }

    Column(
        Modifier.fillMaxSize().background(MaterialTheme.colorScheme.background)
            .verticalScroll(rememberScrollState()).padding(28.dp),
        horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally,
        verticalArrangement = Arrangement.Center
    ) {
        Logo(Modifier.fillMaxWidth(0.6f).aspectRatio(1f))
        Spacer(Modifier.height(14.dp))
        Text("ВМС Групп Chat", fontSize = 26.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
            color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.primary)
        Text(if (register) "Создайте аккаунт" else "Войдите в аккаунт",
            color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onBackground.copy(alpha = 0.6f))
        Spacer(Modifier.height(22.dp))
        if (register) {
            OutlinedTextField(name, { name = it }, label = { Text("Ваше имя") }, singleLine = true,
                shape = RoundedCornerShape(14.dp), modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth())
            Spacer(Modifier.height(10.dp))
        }
        OutlinedTextField(email, { email = it }, label = { Text("Почта") }, singleLine = true,
            shape = RoundedCornerShape(14.dp), modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth())
        Spacer(Modifier.height(10.dp))
        OutlinedTextField(pass, { pass = it }, label = { Text("Пароль (от 6 символов)") }, singleLine = true,
            visualTransformation = PasswordVisualTransformation(),
            shape = RoundedCornerShape(14.dp), modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth())
        if (error.isNotEmpty()) {
            Spacer(Modifier.height(8.dp))
            Text(error, color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.error, fontSize = 13.sp)
        }
        Spacer(Modifier.height(18.dp))
        Button(onClick = { submit() }, enabled = !busy, shape = RoundedCornerShape(14.dp),
            colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(containerColor = Navy),
            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().height(52.dp)
        ) { Text(if (register) "Зарегистрироваться" else "Войти", fontSize = 16.sp, color = Color.White) }
        TextButton(onClick = { register = !register; error = "" }) {
            Text(if (register) "Уже есть аккаунт? Войти" else "Нет аккаунта? Регистрация")
        }
    }
}

// ---------- список чатов ----------

@Composable
fun Chip(label: String, selected: Boolean, onClick: () -> Unit) {
    Text(label, fontSize = 14.sp,
        color = if (selected) Navy else MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onBackground.copy(alpha = 0.8f),
        modifier = Modifier.clip(RoundedCornerShape(18.dp))
            .background(if (selected) Color(0xFFBFE3EA) else MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onBackground.copy(alpha = 0.08f))
            .clickable { onClick() }.padding(horizontal = 14.dp, vertical = 7.dp))
}

@OptIn(ExperimentalFoundationApi::class)
@Composable
fun ChatRow(r: Room, uid: String, now: Long, onLong: () -> Unit = {}, onClick: () -> Unit) {
    val other = if (r.type == "direct") Users[r.members.firstOrNull { it != uid }] else null
    val title = roomTitle(r, uid)
    val preview = when {
        r.lastTime == 0L -> when {
            r.type == "direct" -> if (isOnline(other, now)) "в сети" else "не в сети"
            r.id == "general" -> "Все сотрудники"
            else -> "Участников: ${r.members.size}"
        }
        r.lastUid == uid -> "Вы: ${r.lastText}"
        r.type == "direct" -> r.lastText
        else -> "${r.lastName}: ${r.lastText}"
    }
    Row(Modifier.fillMaxWidth().combinedClickable(onClick = onClick, onLongClick = onLong)
        .padding(horizontal = 16.dp, vertical = 10.dp),
        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
        Avatar(title, if (r.type == "direct") other?.photo ?: "" else r.photo, 52.dp, if (r.type == "direct") isOnline(other, now) else null)
        Spacer(Modifier.width(14.dp))
        Column(Modifier.weight(1f)) {
            Text(title, fontSize = 17.sp, maxLines = 1, overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis,
                fontWeight = if (r.unread > 0) FontWeight.Bold else FontWeight.Medium)
            Text(preview, fontSize = 14.sp, maxLines = 1, overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis,
                color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onBackground.copy(alpha = if (r.unread > 0) 0.85f else 0.55f))
        }
        Spacer(Modifier.width(8.dp))
        Column(horizontalAlignment = Alignment.End) {
            Text(listTime(r.lastTime), fontSize = 12.sp,
                color = if (r.unread > 0) Aqua else MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onBackground.copy(alpha = 0.5f))
            Spacer(Modifier.height(4.dp))
            if (r.unread > 0) {
                Box(Modifier.defaultMinSize(minWidth = 22.dp, minHeight = 22.dp).clip(CircleShape).background(Aqua)
                    .padding(horizontal = 6.dp), contentAlignment = Alignment.Center) {
                    Text(if (r.unread > 99) "99+" else r.unread.toString(), color = Color.White,
                        fontSize = 12.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                }
            } else if (r.id == "general") {
                Text("📌", fontSize = 12.sp)
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
fun RoomsScreen(uid: String, myName: String, general: Room, rooms: List<Room>, onOpen: (Room) -> Unit,
                onProfile: () -> Unit, onDirect: () -> Unit, onGroup: () -> Unit, onAssistant: () -> Unit = {}, onLegal: () -> Unit = {}, onMail: () -> Unit = {}) {
    val ctx = LocalContext.current
    val now = rememberNow()
    var showJoin by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var menu by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var code by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
    var query by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
    var filter by remember { mutableStateOf(0) }
    val amAdmin = Users[uid]?.admin == true
    var menuRoom by remember { mutableStateOf<Room?>(null) }
    var renameRoom by remember { mutableStateOf<Room?>(null) }
    var confirmRoom by remember { mutableStateOf<Pair<Room, String>?>(null) }

    fun join() {
        if (code.isBlank()) return
        Firebase.firestore.collection("rooms").document(code.trim())
            .update("members", FieldValue.arrayUnion(uid))
            .addOnSuccessListener { showJoin = false; code = ""; Toast.makeText(ctx, "Вы в чате", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show() }
            .addOnFailureListener { Toast.makeText(ctx, "Не удалось: проверьте код", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show() }
    }

    val all = listOf(general) + rooms.sortedByDescending { it.lastTime }
    val unreadCount = all.count { it.unread > 0 }
    val groupCount = all.count { it.type != "direct" }
    val shown = all.filter { r ->
        (filter == 0 || (filter == 1 && r.unread > 0) || (filter == 2 && r.type != "direct")) &&
            (query.isBlank() || roomTitle(r, uid).contains(query.trim(), ignoreCase = true))
    }

    Scaffold(
        topBar = {
            Surface(color = Navy, shadowElevation = 4.dp) {
                Row(Modifier.fillMaxWidth().windowInsetsPadding(WindowInsets.statusBars)
                    .padding(start = 12.dp, end = 8.dp, top = 8.dp, bottom = 10.dp),
                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                    Logo(Modifier.size(52.dp))
                    Spacer(Modifier.width(10.dp))
                    Column(Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                        Text("ВМС Групп Chat", color = Color.White, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                            fontSize = 19.sp, maxLines = 1)
                        Spacer(Modifier.height(4.dp))
                        RatesLine()
                    }
                    Box {
                        Box(Modifier.size(40.dp).clip(CircleShape).background(Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.14f))
                            .clickable { menu = true }, contentAlignment = Alignment.Center) {
                            Text("＋", color = Color.White, fontSize = 24.sp)
                        }
                        DropdownMenu(expanded = menu, onDismissRequest = { menu = false }) {
                            DropdownMenuItem(text = { Text("Новый чат") }, onClick = { menu = false; onDirect() })
                            DropdownMenuItem(text = { Text("Новая группа") }, onClick = { menu = false; onGroup() })
                            DropdownMenuItem(text = { Text("Вход по коду") }, onClick = { menu = false; showJoin = true })
                            DropdownMenuItem(text = { Text("Написать письмо") }, onClick = { menu = false; onMail() })
                        }
                    }
                    Spacer(Modifier.width(10.dp))
                    Box(Modifier.clickable { onProfile() }) {
                        Avatar(myName, Users[uid]?.photo ?: "", 42.dp)
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    ) { pad ->
        LazyColumn(Modifier.fillMaxSize().padding(pad)) {
            item {
                OutlinedTextField(query, { query = it }, singleLine = true,
                    placeholder = { Text("Поиск") }, shape = RoundedCornerShape(26.dp),
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(horizontal = 12.dp, vertical = 8.dp))
            }
            item {
                Row(Modifier.padding(horizontal = 12.dp, vertical = 4.dp), horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)) {
                    Chip("Все", filter == 0) { filter = 0 }
                    Chip(if (unreadCount > 0) "Непрочитанное $unreadCount" else "Непрочитанное", filter == 1) { filter = 1 }
                    Chip("Группы $groupCount", filter == 2) { filter = 2 }
                }
            }
            if (query.isBlank() || "помощник".contains(query.trim(), ignoreCase = true)) item {
                Row(Modifier.fillMaxWidth().clickable { onAssistant() }.padding(horizontal = 14.dp, vertical = 10.dp),
                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                    Box(Modifier.size(48.dp).clip(CircleShape).background(Navy), contentAlignment = Alignment.Center) {
                        Text("🤖", fontSize = 24.sp)
                    }
                    Spacer(Modifier.width(12.dp))
                    Column {
                        Text("Помощник ВМС", fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, fontSize = 16.sp)
                        Text("Доверенности, КФК, образцы документов", fontSize = 13.sp,
                            color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onBackground.copy(alpha = 0.6f))
                    }
                }
            }
            if (query.isBlank() || "юридический".contains(query.trim(), ignoreCase = true)) item {
                LegalRow(canDecide = isLawyer(uid) || Users[uid]?.admin == true, onClick = onLegal)
            }
            if (shown.isEmpty()) item { Text("Ничего не найдено", Modifier.padding(20.dp)) }
            items(shown) { r ->
                ChatRow(r, uid, now, onLong = { if (amAdmin) menuRoom = r }) { onOpen(r) }
            }
        }
    }

    menuRoom?.let { r ->
        AlertDialog(
            onDismissRequest = { menuRoom = null },
            title = { Text(roomTitle(r, uid)) },
            text = {
                Column {
                    if (r.type == "group") MenuItem("✏️  Переименовать") { renameRoom = r; menuRoom = null }
                    MenuItem("🧹  Очистить переписку") { confirmRoom = r to "clear"; menuRoom = null }
                    if (r.id != "general") MenuItem("🗑  Удалить чат", Color(0xFFD32F2F)) { confirmRoom = r to "delete"; menuRoom = null }
                }
            },
            confirmButton = { TextButton(onClick = { menuRoom = null }) { Text("Закрыть") } }
        )
    }
    renameRoom?.let { r ->
        var nn by remember(r.id) { mutableStateOf(r.name) }
        AlertDialog(
            onDismissRequest = { renameRoom = null },
            title = { Text("Переименовать") },
            text = { OutlinedTextField(nn, { nn = it }, singleLine = true, label = { Text("Название чата") }) },
            confirmButton = {
                TextButton(onClick = {
                    if (nn.isNotBlank()) Firebase.firestore.collection("rooms").document(r.id).update("name", nn.trim())
                        .addOnFailureListener { Toast.makeText(ctx, "Не удалось переименовать", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show() }
                    renameRoom = null
                }) { Text("Сохранить") }
            },
            dismissButton = { TextButton(onClick = { renameRoom = null }) { Text("Отмена") } }
        )
    }
    confirmRoom?.let { (r, act) ->
        AlertDialog(
            onDismissRequest = { confirmRoom = null },
            title = { Text(if (act == "delete") "Удалить чат?" else "Очистить переписку?") },
            text = { Text(if (act == "delete") "Чат и вся переписка удалятся у всех участников. Это нельзя отменить."
                          else "Все сообщения будут удалены у всех. Это нельзя отменить.") },
            confirmButton = {
                TextButton(onClick = {
                    confirmRoom = null
                    val done: (Boolean) -> Unit = { ok ->
                        if (!ok) Toast.makeText(ctx, "Не удалось выполнить", Toast.LENGTH_LONG).show()
                    }
                    if (act == "delete") deleteRoom(r.id, done) else clearMessages(r, done)
                }) { Text(if (act == "delete") "Удалить" else "Очистить", color = Color(0xFFD32F2F)) }
            },
            dismissButton = { TextButton(onClick = { confirmRoom = null }) { Text("Отмена") } }
        )
    }

    if (showJoin) {
        AlertDialog(
            onDismissRequest = { showJoin = false },
            title = { Text("Вход по коду") },
            text = { OutlinedTextField(code, { code = it }, singleLine = true, label = { Text("Код приглашения") }) },
            confirmButton = { TextButton(onClick = { join() }) { Text("Войти") } },
            dismissButton = { TextButton(onClick = { showJoin = false }) { Text("Отмена") } }
        )
    }
}

// ---------- профиль ----------

@Composable
fun ProfileScreen(uid: String, myName: String, onBack: (() -> Unit)?, onLogout: () -> Unit) {
    val ctx = LocalContext.current
    val picker = rememberLauncherForActivityResult(ActivityResultContracts.GetContent()) { uri: Uri? ->
        if (uri != null) {
            val b64 = encodePhoto(ctx, uri)
            if (b64 != null) {
                Firebase.firestore.collection("users").document(uid).set(mapOf("photo" to b64), SetOptions.merge())
            } else Toast.makeText(ctx, "Не удалось загрузить фото", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
        }
    }
    Scaffold(topBar = { Bar(onBack = onBack) { Text("Профиль") } }) { pad ->
        Column(Modifier.fillMaxSize().padding(pad).padding(24.dp), horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally) {
            Spacer(Modifier.height(16.dp))
            Box(Modifier.clickable { picker.launch("image/*") }) { Avatar(myName, Users[uid]?.photo ?: "", 140.dp) }
            Spacer(Modifier.height(14.dp))
            Text(myName, fontSize = 22.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
            Text(Users[uid]?.email ?: "", fontSize = 14.sp, color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onBackground.copy(alpha = 0.6f))
            Spacer(Modifier.height(12.dp))
            var phone by remember(Users[uid]?.phone) { mutableStateOf(Users[uid]?.phone ?: "") }
            OutlinedTextField(phone, { phone = it }, singleLine = true, label = { Text("Мой телефон") },
                shape = RoundedCornerShape(14.dp), modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth())
            Button(onClick = {
                Firebase.firestore.collection("users").document(uid).set(mapOf("phone" to phone.trim()), SetOptions.merge())
                    .addOnSuccessListener { Toast.makeText(ctx, "Телефон сохранён", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show() }
            }, enabled = phone.trim() != (Users[uid]?.phone ?: ""), shape = RoundedCornerShape(12.dp),
                modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 6.dp)) { Text("Сохранить телефон") }
            Spacer(Modifier.height(18.dp))
            Button(onClick = { picker.launch("image/*") }, shape = RoundedCornerShape(12.dp),
                colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(containerColor = Ocean)) { Text("Выбрать фото", color = Color.White) }
            Spacer(Modifier.height(10.dp))
            OutlinedButton(onClick = onLogout, shape = RoundedCornerShape(12.dp)) { Text("Выйти из аккаунта") }
        }
    }
}

// ---------- новый личный чат / группа ----------

@Composable
fun DirectScreen(uid: String, myName: String, onBack: () -> Unit, onOpen: (String) -> Unit) {
    val now = rememberNow()
    val list = staff().filter { it.uid != uid }.sortedBy { it.name.lowercase() }
    fun open(u: User) {
        val id = listOf(uid, u.uid).sorted().joinToString("_")
        val ref = Firebase.firestore.collection("rooms").document(id)
        ref.get().addOnSuccessListener { d ->
            if (d.exists()) onOpen(id)
            else ref.set(mapOf("type" to "direct", "name" to "", "members" to listOf(uid, u.uid)))
                .addOnSuccessListener { onOpen(id) }
        }
    }
    Scaffold(topBar = { Bar(onBack = onBack) { Text("Новый чат") } }) { pad ->
        LazyColumn(Modifier.fillMaxSize().padding(pad)) {
            if (list.isEmpty()) item { Text("Других сотрудников пока нет", Modifier.padding(20.dp)) }
            items(list) { u ->
                Row(Modifier.fillMaxWidth().clickable { open(u) }.padding(horizontal = 16.dp, vertical = 10.dp),
                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                    Avatar(u.name, u.photo, 46.dp, isOnline(u, now))
                    Spacer(Modifier.width(14.dp))
                    Text(u.name, fontSize = 17.sp)
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
fun GroupScreen(uid: String, onBack: () -> Unit, onCreated: (String) -> Unit) {
    var name by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
    val picked = remember { mutableStateListOf<String>() }
    val list = staff().filter { it.uid != uid }.sortedBy { it.name.lowercase() }
    fun create() {
        val ref = Firebase.firestore.collection("rooms").document()
        ref.set(mapOf("type" to "group", "name" to name.trim(), "members" to (picked.toList() + uid)))
            .addOnSuccessListener { onCreated(ref.id) }
    }
    Scaffold(topBar = { Bar(onBack = onBack) { Text("Новая группа") } }) { pad ->
        Column(Modifier.fillMaxSize().padding(pad)) {
            OutlinedTextField(name, { name = it }, label = { Text("Название группы") }, singleLine = true,
                shape = RoundedCornerShape(14.dp), modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(12.dp))
            Text("Участники", Modifier.padding(horizontal = 16.dp), fontWeight = FontWeight.Medium)
            LazyColumn(Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                items(list) { u ->
                    val on = u.uid in picked
                    Row(Modifier.fillMaxWidth().clickable { if (on) picked.remove(u.uid) else picked.add(u.uid) }
                        .padding(horizontal = 16.dp, vertical = 6.dp), verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                        Checkbox(checked = on, onCheckedChange = { if (on) picked.remove(u.uid) else picked.add(u.uid) })
                        Avatar(u.name, u.photo, 40.dp)
                        Spacer(Modifier.width(12.dp))
                        Text(u.name, fontSize = 16.sp)
                    }
                }
            }
            Button(onClick = { create() }, enabled = name.isNotBlank() && picked.isNotEmpty(),
                shape = RoundedCornerShape(14.dp), colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(containerColor = Navy),
                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(12.dp).height(50.dp)) { Text("Создать группу", color = Color.White) }
        }
    }
}

// ---------- управление чатом (админ) ----------

fun clearMessages(r: Room, done: (Boolean) -> Unit) {
    val db = Firebase.firestore
    fun finish() {
        val ref = if (r.id == "general") db.collection("meta").document("general") else db.collection("rooms").document(r.id)
        ref.update(mapOf<String, Any>("lastText" to "", "lastTime" to 0L, "lastName" to "", "lastUid" to ""))
            .addOnSuccessListener { done(true) }.addOnFailureListener { done(true) }
    }
    fun step() {
        msgCol(r).limit(400).get().addOnSuccessListener { s ->
            if (s.isEmpty) finish()
            else {
                val b = db.batch()
                s.documents.forEach { b.delete(it.reference) }
                b.commit().addOnSuccessListener { step() }.addOnFailureListener { done(false) }
            }
        }.addOnFailureListener { done(false) }
    }
    step()
}

fun deleteRoom(id: String, done: (Boolean) -> Unit) {
    val db = Firebase.firestore
    val msgs = db.collection("rooms").document(id).collection("messages")
    fun step() {
        msgs.limit(400).get().addOnSuccessListener { s ->
            if (s.isEmpty) {
                db.collection("rooms").document(id).delete()
                    .addOnSuccessListener { done(true) }.addOnFailureListener { done(false) }
            } else {
                val b = db.batch()
                s.documents.forEach { b.delete(it.reference) }
                b.commit().addOnSuccessListener { step() }.addOnFailureListener { done(false) }
            }
        }.addOnFailureListener { done(false) }
    }
    step()
}

@Composable
fun RoomManage(room: Room, onDeleted: () -> Unit) {
    val ctx = LocalContext.current
    var name by remember(room.id, room.name) { mutableStateOf(room.name) }
    var confirm by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    val ref = Firebase.firestore.collection("rooms").document(room.id)
    val picker = rememberLauncherForActivityResult(ActivityResultContracts.GetContent()) { uri: Uri? ->
        if (uri != null) {
            val b64 = encodePhoto(ctx, uri)
            if (b64 != null) ref.update("photo", b64)
                .addOnFailureListener { Toast.makeText(ctx, "Не удалось сохранить фото", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show() }
            else Toast.makeText(ctx, "Фото не читается", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
        }
    }
    Column(Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(16.dp)) {
        Text("Управление чатом (админ)", fontWeight = FontWeight.Medium, color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.primary)
        if (room.type == "group") {
            Spacer(Modifier.height(10.dp))
            Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                Avatar(room.name, room.photo, 64.dp)
                Spacer(Modifier.width(14.dp))
                Column {
                    Button(onClick = { picker.launch("image/*") }, shape = RoundedCornerShape(10.dp)) { Text("Сменить фото") }
                    if (room.photo.isNotBlank()) TextButton(onClick = { ref.update("photo", "") }) { Text("Убрать фото") }
                }
            }
            Spacer(Modifier.height(10.dp))
            OutlinedTextField(name, { name = it }, label = { Text("Название чата") }, singleLine = true,
                shape = RoundedCornerShape(14.dp), modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth())
            Button(onClick = {
                ref.update("name", name.trim())
                    .addOnSuccessListener { Toast.makeText(ctx, "Название сохранено", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show() }
                    .addOnFailureListener { Toast.makeText(ctx, "Не удалось сохранить", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show() }
            }, enabled = name.isNotBlank() && name.trim() != room.name, shape = RoundedCornerShape(10.dp),
                modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 8.dp)) { Text("Сохранить название") }
        }
        Spacer(Modifier.height(10.dp))
        OutlinedButton(onClick = { confirm = true }, shape = RoundedCornerShape(10.dp),
            colors = ButtonDefaults.outlinedButtonColors(contentColor = Color(0xFFD32F2F))) { Text("🗑  Удалить чат") }
    }
    if (confirm) {
        AlertDialog(
            onDismissRequest = { confirm = false },
            title = { Text("Удалить чат?") },
            text = { Text("Чат и вся переписка удалятся у всех участников. Это нельзя отменить.") },
            confirmButton = {
                TextButton(onClick = {
                    confirm = false
                    deleteRoom(room.id) { ok ->
                        if (ok) onDeleted()
                        else Toast.makeText(ctx, "Не удалось удалить", Toast.LENGTH_LONG).show()
                    }
                }) { Text("Удалить", color = Color(0xFFD32F2F)) }
            },
            dismissButton = { TextButton(onClick = { confirm = false }) { Text("Отмена") } }
        )
    }
}

// ---------- чат ----------

fun replyPreview(m: Msg): String = when {
    m.text.isNotEmpty() -> m.text.take(100)
    m.image.isNotEmpty() -> "📷 Фото"
    else -> "📎 " + m.fileName
}

@Composable
fun MenuItem(label: String, color: Color = Color.Unspecified, onClick: () -> Unit) {
    Text(label, fontSize = 17.sp, color = color,
        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().clickable { onClick() }.padding(vertical = 12.dp, horizontal = 4.dp))
}

@Suppress("UNCHECKED_CAST")
@OptIn(ExperimentalFoundationApi::class)
@Composable
fun ChatScreen(room: Room, uid: String, myName: String, targets: List<Room>,
               onBack: () -> Unit, onMembers: () -> Unit,
               onOpenRoom: (String) -> Unit = {}, onMailTo: (String) -> Unit = {}) {
    val ctx = LocalContext.current
    var cardUser by remember { mutableStateOf<User?>(null) }
    val clipboard = LocalClipboardManager.current
    val lifecycle = LocalLifecycleOwner.current.lifecycle
    val messages = remember(room.id) { mutableStateListOf<Msg>() }
    var input by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
    var attach by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var mailDlg by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var mailTo by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
    var mailSubj by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
    val draftFiles = remember { mutableStateListOf<MailFile>() }
    var big by remember { mutableStateOf<ImageBitmap?>(null) }
    var menuMsg by remember { mutableStateOf<Msg?>(null) }
    val mentionColor = Color(0xFF1E88E5)
    var replyTo by remember { mutableStateOf<Msg?>(null) }
    var editing by remember { mutableStateOf<Msg?>(null) }
    var deleting by remember { mutableStateOf<Msg?>(null) }
    var forwarding by remember { mutableStateOf<Msg?>(null) }
    var viewers by remember { mutableStateOf<Msg?>(null) }
    val col = remember(room.id) { msgCol(room) }
    val dark = isSystemInDarkTheme()
    val now = rememberNow()
    val title = roomTitle(room, uid)
    val other = if (room.type == "direct") Users[room.members.firstOrNull { it != uid }] else null
    val isAdmin = Users[uid]?.admin == true
    val onlineCount = if (room.id == "general") staff().count { isOnline(it, now) }
        else room.members.count { isOnline(Users[it], now) }
    val others = if (room.id == "general") staff().map { it.uid }.filter { it != uid }
        else room.members.filter { it != uid }

    DisposableEffect(room.id) {
        val reg = col.orderBy("time", Query.Direction.DESCENDING).limit(200)
            .addSnapshotListener { snap, _ ->
                messages.clear()
                snap?.documents?.forEach {
                    messages.add(Msg(it.id, it.getString("text") ?: "", it.getString("uid") ?: "",
                        it.getString("name") ?: "", it.getLong("time") ?: 0L,
                        it.getString("image") ?: "", it.getString("file") ?: "",
                        it.getString("fileName") ?: "",
                        (it.get("readBy") as? List<String>) ?: emptyList(),
                        it.getString("replyName") ?: "", it.getString("replyText") ?: "",
                        it.getString("fwd") ?: "", it.getBoolean("edited") ?: false))
                }
            }
        onDispose { reg.remove() }
    }

    LaunchedEffect(room.id, room.unread) { if (room.unread > 0) markRead(room, uid) }

    // отмечаем чужие сообщения прочитанными, пока чат открыт на экране
    val unreadIds = messages.filter { it.uid != uid && uid !in it.readBy }.map { it.id }
    LaunchedEffect(unreadIds) {
        if (unreadIds.isNotEmpty() && lifecycle.currentState.isAtLeast(Lifecycle.State.RESUMED)) {
            unreadIds.take(50).forEach { col.document(it).update("readBy", FieldValue.arrayUnion(uid)) }
        }
    }

    fun sendMsg(text: String, image: String, file: String, fileName: String, preview: String) {
        val time = System.currentTimeMillis()
        val m = hashMapOf<String, Any>("text" to text, "uid" to uid, "name" to myName, "time" to time)
        if (image.isNotEmpty()) m["image"] = image
        if (file.isNotEmpty()) { m["file"] = file; m["fileName"] = fileName }
        replyTo?.let { m["replyName"] = it.name; m["replyText"] = replyPreview(it) }
        col.add(m).addOnFailureListener { Toast.makeText(ctx, "Сообщение не отправлено", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show() }
        bumpLast(room, uid, myName, preview, time)
        replyTo = null
    }

    fun send() {
        val t = input.trim()
        if (t.isEmpty()) return
        val e = editing
        if (e != null) {
            col.document(e.id).update(mapOf("text" to t, "edited" to true))
                .addOnFailureListener { Toast.makeText(ctx, "Не удалось изменить", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show() }
            editing = null
        } else sendMsg(t, "", "", "", t)
        input = ""
    }

    fun forward(m: Msg, target: Room) {
        val time = System.currentTimeMillis()
        val d = hashMapOf<String, Any>("text" to m.text, "uid" to uid, "name" to myName, "time" to time,
            "fwd" to m.fwd.ifEmpty { m.name })
        if (m.image.isNotEmpty()) d["image"] = m.image
        if (m.file.isNotEmpty()) { d["file"] = m.file; d["fileName"] = m.fileName }
        msgCol(target).add(d)
            .addOnSuccessListener { Toast.makeText(ctx, "Переслано", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show() }
            .addOnFailureListener { Toast.makeText(ctx, "Не удалось переслать", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show() }
        bumpLast(target, uid, myName, "↪ " + replyPreview(m), time)
    }

    val photoPicker = rememberLauncherForActivityResult(ActivityResultContracts.GetContent()) { uri: Uri? ->
        if (uri != null) {
            val b = encodeChatImage(ctx, uri)
            if (b != null) sendMsg("", b, "", "", "📷 Фото")
            else Toast.makeText(ctx, "Не удалось загрузить фото", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
        }
    }
    val docPicker = rememberLauncherForActivityResult(ActivityResultContracts.GetContent()) { uri: Uri? ->
        if (uri != null) {
            val d = readDoc(ctx, uri)
            if (d != null) sendMsg("", "", d.second, d.first, "📎 " + d.first)
            else Toast.makeText(ctx, "Файл больше 600 КБ или не читается", Toast.LENGTH_LONG).show()
        }
    }

    val mailPicker = rememberLauncherForActivityResult(ActivityResultContracts.GetMultipleContents()) { uris: List<Uri> ->
        uris.forEach { draftFiles.add(MailFile(it, fileNameOf(ctx, it))) }
    }
    cardUser?.let { u -> ContactDialog(u, uid,
        onChat = { cardUser = null; if (room.type == "direct" && u.uid == other?.uid) {} else openDirect(uid, u, onOpenRoom) },
        onMail = { cardUser = null; onMailTo(u.email) }, onDismiss = { cardUser = null }) }
    if (mailDlg) {
        AlertDialog(
            onDismissRequest = { mailDlg = false },
            title = { Text("Отправить по почте") },
            text = {
                Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)) {
                    OutlinedTextField(mailTo, { mailTo = it }, label = { Text("Кому (e-mail)") }, singleLine = true)
                    OutlinedTextField(mailSubj, { mailSubj = it }, label = { Text("Тема") }, singleLine = true)
                    Text("Текст: " + (if (input.isBlank()) "пусто" else input.take(60)) +
                        "\nФайлов: " + draftFiles.size, fontSize = 13.sp)
                }
            },
            confirmButton = {
                TextButton(onClick = {
                    mailDlg = false
                    sendMail(ctx, mailTo, mailSubj, input, draftFiles.toList())
                }) { Text("Открыть почту") }
            },
            dismissButton = { TextButton(onClick = { mailDlg = false }) { Text("Отмена") } }
        )
    }

    Scaffold(
        topBar = {
            Bar(onBack = onBack, actions = {
                TextButton(onClick = onMembers,
                    colors = ButtonDefaults.textButtonColors(contentColor = Color.White)) { Text("👥 $onlineCount", fontSize = 16.sp) }
            }) {
                Row(Modifier.clickable(enabled = room.type == "direct" && other != null) { cardUser = other },
                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                    Avatar(title, if (room.type == "direct") other?.photo ?: "" else room.photo, 38.dp)
                    Spacer(Modifier.width(10.dp))
                    Column {
                        Text(title, fontSize = 17.sp, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                        Text(if (room.type == "direct") (if (isOnline(other, now)) "в сети" else "не в сети")
                            else "в сети: $onlineCount", fontSize = 12.sp, color = Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.75f))
                    }
                }
            }
        },
        bottomBar = {
            Surface(tonalElevation = 3.dp, shadowElevation = 6.dp) {
                Column {
                    val bar: Pair<String, String>? = editing?.let { "Редактирование" to it.text }
                        ?: replyTo?.let { ("Ответ: " + it.name) to replyPreview(it) }
                    if (bar != null) {
                        Row(Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(start = 14.dp, end = 4.dp, top = 8.dp),
                            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                            Box(Modifier.width(3.dp).height(36.dp).background(Aqua))
                            Spacer(Modifier.width(8.dp))
                            Column(Modifier.weight(1f)) {

                                Text(bar.second, fontSize = 13.sp, maxLines = 1, overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis,
                                    color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurface.copy(alpha = 0.7f))
                            }
                            TextButton(onClick = {
                                val wasEdit = editing != null
                                replyTo = null; editing = null
                                if (wasEdit) input = ""
                            }) { Text("✕", fontSize = 18.sp) }
                        }
                    }
                    val atIdx = input.lastIndexOf('@')
                    val atQuery = if (atIdx >= 0 && (atIdx == 0 || input[atIdx - 1].isWhitespace()) &&
                        !input.substring(atIdx + 1).contains('\n') && input.substring(atIdx + 1).count { it == ' ' } <= 1)
                        input.substring(atIdx + 1) else null
                    if (atQuery != null) {
                        val cand = (if (room.type == "direct") listOfNotNull(other) else others.mapNotNull { Users[it] })
                            .filter { it.name.contains(atQuery.trim(), ignoreCase = true) }.sortedBy { it.name }.take(5)
                        if (cand.isNotEmpty()) {
                            Column(Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(horizontal = 8.dp, vertical = 4.dp)) {
                                for (u in cand) {
                                    Row(Modifier.fillMaxWidth().clip(RoundedCornerShape(10.dp))
                                        .clickable { input = input.substring(0, atIdx) + "@" + u.name + " " }
                                        .padding(horizontal = 8.dp, vertical = 6.dp),
                                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                                        Avatar(u.name, u.photo, 30.dp)
                                        Spacer(Modifier.width(10.dp))
                                        Column {
                                            Text(u.name, fontWeight = FontWeight.Medium)
                                            val sub = listOfNotNull(u.position.ifBlank { null }, deptName(u.dept).takeIf { u.dept.isNotBlank() }).joinToString(" · ")
                                            if (sub.isNotBlank()) Text(sub, fontSize = 11.sp,
                                                color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurface.copy(alpha = 0.6f))
                                        }
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    if (draftFiles.isNotEmpty()) {
                        Row(Modifier.fillMaxWidth().horizontalScroll(rememberScrollState()).padding(horizontal = 10.dp, vertical = 4.dp),
                            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                            draftFiles.forEachIndexed { i, f ->
                                Surface(shape = RoundedCornerShape(14.dp), tonalElevation = 6.dp,
                                    modifier = Modifier.padding(end = 6.dp)) {
                                    Row(Modifier.padding(start = 10.dp), verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                                        Text("📎 " + f.name.take(24), fontSize = 12.sp, maxLines = 1)
                                        TextButton(onClick = { draftFiles.removeAt(i) },
                                            contentPadding = PaddingValues(horizontal = 8.dp)) { Text("✕", fontSize = 13.sp) }
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    Row(Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(8.dp), verticalAlignment = Alignment.Bottom) {
                        Box(Modifier.padding(bottom = 4.dp)) {
                            Box(Modifier.size(48.dp).clip(CircleShape).clickable { attach = true },
                                contentAlignment = Alignment.Center) { Text("📎", fontSize = 24.sp) }
                            DropdownMenu(expanded = attach, onDismissRequest = { attach = false }) {
                                DropdownMenuItem(text = { Text("Фото") }, onClick = { attach = false; photoPicker.launch("image/*") })
                                DropdownMenuItem(text = { Text("Документ") }, onClick = { attach = false; docPicker.launch("*/*") })
                                DropdownMenuItem(text = { Text("✉️ Файлы для письма (не отправлять в чат)") },
                                    onClick = { attach = false; mailPicker.launch("*/*") })
                            }
                        }
                        OutlinedTextField(input, { input = it }, Modifier.weight(1f),
                            placeholder = { Text("Сообщение") }, shape = RoundedCornerShape(26.dp), maxLines = 5)
                        Spacer(Modifier.width(4.dp))
                        Box(Modifier.padding(bottom = 4.dp)) {
                            Box(Modifier.size(44.dp).clip(CircleShape).clickable {
                                if (mailTo.isBlank()) mailTo = other?.email ?: ""
                                if (mailSubj.isBlank()) mailSubj = "ВМС Групп"
                                mailDlg = true
                            }, contentAlignment = Alignment.Center) { Text("✉️", fontSize = 22.sp) }
                        }
                        Spacer(Modifier.width(4.dp))
                        Box(Modifier.size(54.dp).clip(CircleShape)
                            .background(if (input.isBlank()) Color.Gray else Aqua)
                            .clickable(enabled = input.isNotBlank()) { send() },
                            contentAlignment = Alignment.Center) { Text(if (editing != null) "✓" else "➤", color = Color.White, fontSize = 20.sp) }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    ) { pad ->
        Box(Modifier.fillMaxSize().padding(pad)) {
            if (messages.isEmpty()) {
                Text("Сообщений пока нет.\nНапишите первым!", Modifier.align(Alignment.Center),
                    textAlign = TextAlign.Center,
                    color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onBackground.copy(alpha = 0.5f))
            }
            LazyColumn(Modifier.fillMaxSize(), reverseLayout = true,
                contentPadding = PaddingValues(horizontal = 10.dp, vertical = 8.dp)) {
                itemsIndexed(messages) { i, m ->
                    val older = messages.getOrNull(i + 1)
                    val newDay = older == null || !sameDay(older.time, m.time)
                    val newSender = newDay || older?.uid != m.uid
                    val mine = m.uid == uid
                    Column(Modifier.fillMaxWidth()) {
                        if (newDay) {
                            Box(Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(vertical = 8.dp), contentAlignment = Alignment.Center) {
                                Text(dayLabel(m.time), fontSize = 12.sp,
                                    color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onBackground.copy(alpha = 0.7f),
                                    modifier = Modifier.clip(RoundedCornerShape(12.dp))
                                        .background(MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onBackground.copy(alpha = 0.08f))
                                        .padding(horizontal = 12.dp, vertical = 4.dp))
                            }

                            horizontalArrangement = if (mine) Arrangement.End else Arrangement.Start,

                                if (newSender) Box(Modifier.clickable { cardUser = Users[m.uid] }) { Avatar(m.name, Users[m.uid]?.photo ?: "", 32.dp) }
                                else Spacer(Modifier.width(32.dp))
                                Spacer(Modifier.width(6.dp))
                            }

                                RoundedCornerShape(16.dp, 16.dp, if (mine) 4.dp else 16.dp, if (mine) 16.dp else 4.dp)
                            ).background(
                                if (mine) (if (dark) Color(0xFF1E4D7A) else Color(0xFFCFE3F7))
                                else MaterialTheme.colorScheme.surface
                            ).combinedClickable(onClick = { menuMsg = m }, onLongClick = { menuMsg = m })
                                .padding(horizontal = 12.dp, vertical = 8.dp)) {

                                        color = NameColors[(m.name.hashCode() and 0x7fffffff) % NameColors.size])
                                }
                                if (m.fwd.isNotEmpty()) {
                                    Text("↪ Переслано от " + m.fwd, fontSize = 12.sp, fontStyle = FontStyle.Italic,
                                        color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurface.copy(alpha = 0.6f))

                                        .background(Color.Black.copy(alpha = 0.07f)).padding(6.dp)) {

                                        Text(m.replyText, fontSize = 13.sp, maxLines = 2, overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis)
                                    }
                                }
                                if (m.image.isNotEmpty()) {
                                    val bmp = remember(m.image) { decodePhoto(m.image) }
                                    if (bmp != null) {
                                        Image(bmp, null, Modifier.widthIn(max = 250.dp).padding(bottom = 4.dp)
                                            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(10.dp))

                                            contentScale = ContentScale.Fit)
                                    }
